FARKLI KÜLTÜRLER, ORTAK BİR YAŞAM — START FRAME PROMPTLARI (Nano Banana 2)
=========================================================================
Dünya: pixar_3d_edu · register: EDU · 53 kare · 5. Sınıf Sosyal Bilgiler 5.1.2
Yasa: agents/PROMPT-YASASI.md · Referanslar: Farklı Kültürler_REFERANSLAR.txt

BASMA SIRASI — referans bağımlılığı:
  @mira2 → @dara → @bahce  inince  S1 (K01-K07) basılabilir
  @sinif inince                     S2 (K08-K14) ve S4 (K24-K30) açılır
  @kaval inince                     S3 (K15-K23) açılır — filmin dönüm noktası
  @dede zaten var (önceki ders)     K18 ve K50

Prompt blokları `-----` ayraçları arasındadır. Başlık, FİKİR ve PLAN satırları
YÖNETMEN İÇİNDİR — motora KOPYALANMAZ.

##### S1 — BAHÇEDEKİ İKİ UÇ (K01–K07) #####

### K01 | VO1 "Bu Mira; okul bahçesi yarınki 23 Nisan şenliği için süsleniyor." · yazı: "23 NİSAN" · KARAKTER
FİKİR: Şenlik yarı kurulu. @mira2 sağlam bir tahta sandalyenin üstünde, çınarın alt dalına flama ipini bağlıyor — düğüm henüz sıkılmamış. İpin öbür ucu hâlâ yerde, kangal hâlinde. Dalların arasından, çok uzakta, bankta oturan birinin dizleri görünüyor; yüz değil, henüz. İp iki uca gidiyor ama bir uç bağlanmamış.
PLAN:  kahraman @mira2 sandalyede ayakta · net insan: BİR · ışık öğleden sonra güneşi çınar yapraklarından süzülüp yalnız kollarına, ipe ve ilk flamaya düşüyor, bahçe zeminine hiç ulaşmıyor · özne zeminden sıcak rim + arkasındaki çınar gövdesinin koyu kütlesiyle ayrılıyor · FEDA: yüzünün yarısı yaprak gölgesinde parçalı kalır
⚠ @mira2 KADRAJ KİLİDİ: referans sayfasının "tam boy, ayakta, dinlenme pozunda" duruşu EZİLİR — burada sandalyenin oturağında ayakta, iki kolu başının üstünde, ağırlığı ön ayak parmaklarında.
-----
[1 LENS]      35mm lens at f/2.8, standing eye level, medium-wide three-quarter view taking @mira2, the low branch she is working on and the depth of the yard beyond — a coil of unused bunting rope lies on the paving in the near lower-left foreground, blurred and sliced by the frame edge
[2 SUBJECT]   — @mira2 standing up on a solid wooden chair with both arms raised to the low branch of the plane tree, winding the bunting cord twice around the bark, the knot started and still slack
[3 IDENTITY]  @mira2 up on the chair seat, weight forward on the balls of her feet, both arms above her head, chin lifted — deliberately not standing at rest on the ground and not in a full-figure studio pose. Nothing above the low branch is in frame; the upper canopy is cut away by the top edge. Her lower lip is caught under her teeth in concentration, one sleeve fallen back to the elbow, her eyes on the crossing of the cord and never on the lens; a broad band of leaf shadow falls across the upper half of her face so only her mouth and jaw are fully lit. Far across the yard, through a gap in the foliage, @dara sits on the bench — only her knees and her hands folded on them show between the leaves, the rest of her body occluded by the branch mass, fully modelled and deeply out of focus, her face not visible at all. All skin in frame renders matte with low specular and is never tinted green or grey; the reference tones are kept exactly as they are.
[4 SETTING]   The setting is @bahce half dressed for tomorrow: the mottled plane tree at the near left, the long green cast-iron bench beneath it, worn concrete slabs with grass in the joints, the beige stucco school building across the yard with the flag of Turkey hanging in soft folds — and on the near branch one single triangular paper pennant already hung, stiff and taut, the rest of the cord still bare. A folding table by the steps is loaded edge to edge with stacked paper pennants, a jar of clothes pegs and a roll of tape, so no bare board or empty panel surface is left anywhere in the frame
[5 LIGHT]     A late-afternoon sun comes through the plane leaves from the upper right; it takes only her forearms, her raised hands, the cord and the face of the first hung pennant, and it reaches nothing of the yard floor, which stays deep cool indigo under the tree
[7 ALIVE]     Three things are alive in the frame: the loose end of the cord swinging below the half-made knot, dappled leaf light crawling across her forearms, and the single hung pennant lifting a few degrees on the air.
[8 DEPTH]     Depth in three layers — the rope coil blurred and cut at the lower left edge, @mira2, the branch and the first pennant sharp, the bench, @dara's occluded knees and the school façade softening into gentle bokeh while staying fully modelled.
[9 CONTACT]   Every object rests in contact with its surface and casts a soft contact shadow — the rope coil flattens where it lies and darkens the slab beneath its lowest turn. Her grip: the cord runs across the pads of her left index and middle fingers with the thumb pinning it against the bark, while her right hand holds the free end low and has not yet pulled it through, the weight of the loop hanging from that right wrist.
STYLE: Feature-animation 3D CGI in the RenderMan / RenderMan-successor premium-CG feature-animation pipeline lineage, subsurface scattering on character skin that carries the scene's light temperature, Every prop has a deliberate overscale factor (10-15%) for child-safe readability, Material specificity is non-negotiable, wood shows visible grain with satin-varnish sheen, fabric shows woven stitch texture and slight fiber fray at edges, complementary bounce fill at 25-35% intensity in the key's opposing hue, FULL 3D CGI FEATURE-ANIMATION RENDER, continuous physically-based shading. Silhouette reads entirely through lighting rim and value separation, Ambient occlusion painted in, never raytraced-hard.
LIGHT AND PALETTE: shadows read as deep cool indigo, midtones read as vivid warm burnt orange, accents read as vivid warm amber, highlights read as near-white warm ivory — palette character: warm-honey SSS dominant on skin; complementary cool-violet bounce in shadow; wood tones in brown-ochre range with satin sheen; fabric in warm-saturated primaries with fiber texture. NO acid green, NO neon, NO teal-orange Hollywood grade, NO desaturated grey anywhere in character or environment. Render these as light behaviour, never flat fills.
TEXT: The carrier is the single triangular pennant already hung on the branch — thick card-weight paper, stiff and pulled taut, its face turned flat and parallel to the sensor · the letterform is screen-printed poster ink on rough paper, the pigment sitting thin where the paper grain rises and thicker in the low spots, the stroke edges faintly feathered into the fibre · it reads "23 NİSAN" — a two-digit numeral and one word of five letters, all capitals, and the second letter of that word is a dotted capital İ · positioned on the pennant in the upper middle of the frame with the branch above it and nothing crossing in front of the letters · ORIENTATION: the words read left-to-right in normal reading direction and are never mirrored, reversed, rotated or flipped; they appear on this one named pennant and on no other surface in the frame · any background sign is soft-focus, Turkish or blank.
NEGATIVE: the lettering appears only on the one hung pennant and on no other pennant and on no other surface — the stacked pennants on the folding table stay blank card seen edge-on; the bunting is only half-strung, the knot stays open and the far end of the rope stays coiled on the ground; @dara's face is never visible and she never turns toward the lens; NO photoreal or live-action capture and no real-human photographic skin — continuous 3D feature-animation CGI throughout; NO hard-black shadow; NO 2D cel shading; NO hard black outlines; NO flat graphic fill; NO clay/plasticine surface texture on character skin; NO outlines; NO lens flare unless motivated by a visible practical light source in frame; NO anamorphic streak; NO hard cel shading, NO cartoon black outline, NO anime-style eye geometry; NO toon shader, NO 2D illustration style on 3D forms; NO teal-orange Hollywood grade, NO desaturated look, NO high-contrast noir; Turkish label only, NO English signage; NO octane-render aesthetic (avoid: harsh physical sky, raw caustics, overly sharp AO); NO photoreal or live-action capture, NO real-human photographic skin — this is continuous dimensional 3D feature-animation CGI in every scene; no recognizable franchise or real-person characters, logos, brand names; no icon, pictogram, chart, diagram, arrow, gauge or infographic panel standing in for the real object or the real action — the literal thing is IN the frame, rendered as matter, not illustrated as a concept; no empty adjectives (cinematic, dynamic, stunning, 4K); no flat slide; no warped or drifting text; no European or Parisian stone architecture, no desert or orientalist styling — this is contemporary Anatolian
-----

### K02 | VO2 "Bahçenin ucundaki bankta, sınıfa yeni gelen bir kız tek başına oturuyor." · yazı: YOK · KARAKTER
🔴 BU AÇILIŞ KADRAJIDIR ve K49 ile BİREBİR AYNI olacak. [1 LENS] ve [5 LIGHT] cümleleri K49'da AYNEN kopyalanır; fark yalnız bankın üstündedir.
FİKİR: Bank beş tahtalı. @dara en uçtaki tahtada oturuyor, çantası yanındaki tahtada; kalan ÜÇ TAHTA boş. Kompozisyonun konusu o boşluk. Çok geride @mira2 hâlâ ipi bağlıyor, sırtı dönük ama tam render. İki uç aynı karede, aralarında bütün bahçe var.
PLAN:  kahraman BANK ve üstündeki üç tahtalık boşluk (kadrajın orta üçte biri) · net insan: İKİ (@dara yüzüyle net; @mira2 geride, sırtı dönük, odak dışı ama TAM RENDER) · ışık yatay geç öğleden sonra ışığı yalnız bankın tahtalarına ve @dara'nın dizlerine düşüyor; çınarın gövdesi ve bankın dökme demir ayakları ışığın hiç ulaşmadığı indigo gölgede · özne ayrımı: gümüşleşmiş bank tahtası koyu gövdenin önünde parlıyor · FEDA: @dara'nın yüzünün üst yarısı yaprak gölgesinde
⚠ @dara KADRAJ KİLİDİ: "tam boy, ayakta, stüdyo" duruşu EZİLİR — burada bankın en ucuna oturmuş, sırtı arkalığa değmiyor, iki eli kucağında.
-----
[1 LENS]      50mm lens at f/2.8, standing eye level, medium-wide view of the bench from the front and slightly to the left, late-afternoon — an out-of-focus shrub branch from the planter run reaches into the lower left foreground and is sliced by the frame edge
[2 SUBJECT]   — the long bench under the plane tree with @dara sitting right at its far end, her satchel set down on the seat beside her, and the whole remaining length of the bench standing empty across the middle third of the frame
[3 IDENTITY]  @dara seated on the very end of the bench, her back not touching the rest of it, knees together and both hands closed in her lap — deliberately not standing at rest and not in a full-figure studio pose; framed from the shins up, no shoes and no paving directly beneath her in frame. Her chin is slightly down and her eyes travel out across the yard, following something she is not walking toward; a band of leaf shadow crosses the upper half of her face so her eyes read in shade while her mouth and jaw stay lit. Far back across the yard, @mira2 stands on a chair at the plane branch with her arms up at the cord, turned away from the lens but fully rendered and softly out of focus — her body complete, her feet on the chair seat, never a flat shape. All skin in frame renders matte with low specular and is never tinted green or grey; the reference tones are kept exactly as they are.
[4 SETTING]   The setting is @bahce: the bench is five worn timber slats gone silver on top, its two cast-iron end frames dark green and rusting at the bolts; beside @dara the seat runs on bare, room enough for three more children, and ends at the near arm. Behind it the mottled plane trunk, the low run of shrubs in their raised concrete planter, worn concrete slabs with grass in the joints, and the beige stucco school building beyond with the flag of Turkey in soft folds; the yard noticeboard by the steps is layered over completely with overlapping sheets of coloured paper and pinned children's paintings so that no bare panel is left anywhere
[5 LIGHT]     A low horizontal late-afternoon light comes in from the left across the yard; it takes only the silvered top slats of the bench and @dara's knees and forearms, and it reaches neither the plane trunk nor the cast-iron bench legs, which stay a deep cool indigo with no highlight of their own
[7 ALIVE]     Three things are alive in the frame: leaf shadow drifting slowly across the empty slats, a loose thread at her cuff moving on the air, and the satchel strap settling by a finger's width as it slides.
[8 DEPTH]     Depth in three layers — the shrub branch blurred and cut at the lower left edge, the bench, the empty seat and @dara sharp across the middle, the plane trunk, @mira2 on her chair and the school façade softening into gentle bokeh while staying fully modelled.
[9 CONTACT]   Every object rests in contact with its surface and casts a soft contact shadow — the satchel slumps against the slats with a dark seam under its base and pushes its buckle down into the gap between two boards. Her hands: fingers laced loosely in her lap, the right thumb held still on top of the left, carrying no weight but their own.
STYLE: Feature-animation 3D CGI in the RenderMan / RenderMan-successor premium-CG feature-animation pipeline lineage, subsurface scattering on character skin that carries the scene's light temperature, Every prop has a deliberate overscale factor (10-15%) for child-safe readability, Material specificity is non-negotiable, wood shows visible grain with satin-varnish sheen, fabric shows woven stitch texture and slight fiber fray at edges, complementary bounce fill at 25-35% intensity in the key's opposing hue, FULL 3D CGI FEATURE-ANIMATION RENDER, continuous physically-based shading. Silhouette reads entirely through lighting rim and value separation, Ambient occlusion painted in, never raytraced-hard.
LIGHT AND PALETTE: shadows read as deep cool indigo, midtones read as vivid warm burnt orange, accents read as vivid warm amber, highlights read as near-white warm ivory — palette character: warm-honey SSS dominant on skin; complementary cool-violet bounce in shadow; wood tones in brown-ochre range with satin sheen; fabric in warm-saturated primaries with fiber texture. NO acid green, NO neon, NO teal-orange Hollywood grade, NO desaturated grey anywhere in character or environment. Render these as light behaviour, never flat fills.
TEXT: No on-screen text is designed into this frame. Every surface in shot that could carry lettering is dressed rather than left bare — panels are layered over with overlapping paper and pinned work, shelves and racks are packed full, cloth is folded or rolled closed, and any remaining surface is given material and grain instead of a blank face, so no empty panel is left where a sign could form. Any background sign is soft-focus, Turkish or blank.
NEGATIVE: nobody else sits on the bench and no additional figures exist beyond @dara and the distant @mira2; the empty stretch of seat stays empty and unbroken and nothing is placed on it; @mira2 stays turned away, fully rendered and out of focus, never a flat shape; @dara does not look at the lens and her mouth stays closed; the bench carries no plaque, no carved initials and no lettering of any kind; NO photoreal or live-action capture and no real-human photographic skin — continuous 3D feature-animation CGI throughout; NO hard-black shadow; NO 2D cel shading; NO hard black outlines; NO flat graphic fill; NO clay/plasticine surface texture on character skin; NO outlines; NO lens flare unless motivated by a visible practical light source in frame; NO anamorphic streak; NO hard cel shading, NO cartoon black outline, NO anime-style eye geometry; NO toon shader, NO 2D illustration style on 3D forms; NO teal-orange Hollywood grade, NO desaturated look, NO high-contrast noir; Turkish label only, NO English signage; NO octane-render aesthetic (avoid: harsh physical sky, raw caustics, overly sharp AO); NO photoreal or live-action capture, NO real-human photographic skin — this is continuous dimensional 3D feature-animation CGI in every scene; no recognizable franchise or real-person characters, logos, brand names; no icon, pictogram, chart, diagram, arrow, gauge or infographic panel standing in for the real object or the real action — the literal thing is IN the frame, rendered as matter, not illustrated as a concept; no empty adjectives (cinematic, dynamic, stunning, 4K); no flat slide; no warped or drifting text; no European or Parisian stone architecture, no desert or orientalist styling — this is contemporary Anatolian

### K03 | VO3 "Adı Dara; ailesi geçen ay Bulgaristan'dan bu mahalleye taşındı." · yazı: "5-B" · KARAKTER
FİKİR: @dara yakın plan. Kucağında yeni bir defter, üstünde henüz sökülmemiş bir taşınma bandının yapışkan izi var. Defteri açmamış; kapağını parmağıyla düzeltiyor. Yeni bir yere yazılmış bir hayat, henüz ilk sayfası açılmamış.
PLAN:  kahraman @dara · net insan: BİR · ışık açık havada çınardan yansıyan sıcak dolaylı ışık yalnız ellerine, defterin kapağına ve çenesine değiyor; saçının üstüne ve arkasındaki duvara ulaşmıyor · ayrım: koyu duvarın önünde açık kapaklı defter · FEDA: gözlerinden biri düşen saçın altında kalır
⚠ @dara KADRAJ KİLİDİ: duruş EZİLİR — bankta oturuyor, öne eğik, defter uyluklarının üstünde; kadraj uyluk hizasından yukarısı, ayak ve zemin görünmez.
-----
[1 LENS]      85mm lens at f/2.0, seated eye level, tight three-quarter view from just off her right shoulder — the near arm of the bench, an iron scroll gone dark, cuts the lower right foreground and is sliced by the frame edge
[2 SUBJECT]   — @dara looking down at a brand-new exercise book lying closed across her thighs, dragging one fingertip along the top edge of its cover to press it flat
[3 IDENTITY]  @dara seated and bent forward over her lap, shoulders rounded, elbows in against her ribs — deliberately not standing at rest and not in a full-figure studio pose; framed from mid-thigh up, no feet, no shoes and no paving visible. A fallen strand of hair hangs across one eye and hides it completely while the other eye stays open and readable; her mouth is closed and her jaw slack, the expression of somebody occupying herself with a small task so as not to be occupied with a large one. Any skin visible in frame renders matte with low specular and is never tinted green or grey; the reference tones are kept exactly as they are.
[4 SETTING]   The setting is @bahce seen close in: the exercise book is a fresh pale-card cover with sharp unhandled corners, and across one corner sits the ghost of a packing-tape strip — a tacky rectangle holding dust and one curled fibre of cardboard. Behind her the mottled plane trunk fills most of the background in dark flaking plates, with the shrub run in its raised concrete planter beyond and the silvered bench slats running out of frame; nothing behind her is a flat wall or a bare panel
[5 LIGHT]     Warm indirect light bounces down off the plane leaves and takes only her hands, the face of the book cover and the underside of her chin, and it reaches neither the top of her hair nor the trunk behind her, which stays deep cool indigo
[7 ALIVE]     Three things are alive in the frame: the hair strand swinging a few millimetres across her hidden eye, a loose fibre standing up off the tape residue, and the top corner of the cover flexing back down under her fingertip.
[8 DEPTH]     Depth in three layers — the dark iron bench arm blurred and cut at the lower right edge, her hands and the book cover sharp, the plane trunk and shrubs dissolving into soft bokeh while staying fully modelled.
[9 CONTACT]   Every object rests in contact with its surface and casts a soft contact shadow — the book lies flat across her thighs and dents the wool of her skirt into a shallow trough along its lower edge, with a dark seam beneath its spine. Her grip: the left palm lies flat on the lower corner holding the book steady, the right index and middle fingertip press and drag along the top edge, and the weight of the book is carried entirely by her thighs, not by her hands.
STYLE: Feature-animation 3D CGI in the RenderMan / RenderMan-successor premium-CG feature-animation pipeline lineage, subsurface scattering on character skin that carries the scene's light temperature, Every prop has a deliberate overscale factor (10-15%) for child-safe readability, Material specificity is non-negotiable, wood shows visible grain with satin-varnish sheen, fabric shows woven stitch texture and slight fiber fray at edges, complementary bounce fill at 25-35% intensity in the key's opposing hue, FULL 3D CGI FEATURE-ANIMATION RENDER, continuous physically-based shading. Silhouette reads entirely through lighting rim and value separation, Ambient occlusion painted in, never raytraced-hard.
LIGHT AND PALETTE: shadows read as deep cool indigo, midtones read as vivid warm burnt orange, accents read as vivid warm amber, highlights read as near-white warm ivory — palette character: warm-honey SSS dominant on skin; complementary cool-violet bounce in shadow; wood tones in brown-ochre range with satin sheen; fabric in warm-saturated primaries with fiber texture. NO acid green, NO neon, NO teal-orange Hollywood grade, NO desaturated grey anywhere in character or environment. Render these as light behaviour, never flat fills.
TEXT: The carrier is the small printed paper label pasted on the front cover of the exercise book along its spine edge, lying flat and turned parallel to the sensor · the letterform is matte press ink on smooth label paper, the pigment sunk very slightly into the stock so the strokes have a soft absorbed edge, one corner of the label lifting a hair off the card · it reads "5-B" — a numeral, a hyphen and one capital letter, three characters in all · positioned on the label in the lower middle of the frame, clear of both her hands so no finger crosses the characters · ORIENTATION: it reads left-to-right in normal reading direction and is never mirrored, reversed, rotated or flipped; it appears on this one named label and on no other surface in the frame · any background sign is soft-focus, Turkish or blank.

### K04 | VO4 "Mira ona bakıyor ama yanına gitmiyor; nedenini kendisi de bilmiyor." · yazı: YOK · KARAKTER
FİKİR: @mira2 düğüm atmayı bırakmış — düğüm yarım, ip parmaklarının arasında gevşek duruyor. Eli işte, gözü bankta. Beden iki yöne bölünmüş. Ön planda, bağlanmamış ip ucu odak dışı sallanıyor ve kadrajı çaprazlıyor.
PLAN:  kahraman @mira2'nin bölünmüş bedeni · net insan: BİR · ışık yalnız elleri ve yarım düğüm üstünde; yüzü ışığın kenarında, gözleri gölgede · ayrım: sıcak ip, arkasındaki soğuk gölgeli duvar önünde · FEDA: yarım düğüm net, yüzü hafif odak dışı
⚠ @mira2 KADRAJ KİLİDİ: duruş EZİLİR — hâlâ sandalyenin üstünde, bir kolu yukarıda dalda, gövdesi dala dönük ama başı tam ters yöne, bankın olduğu tarafa çevrilmiş. Kadraj göğüs ortasından yukarısı; göğsün altındaki hiçbir şey görünmüyor.
-----
[1 LENS]      85mm lens at f/2.0, low angle looking slightly up at her, tight view of the raised hand and the turned head together — the unattached end of the cord swings loose across the near foreground on a diagonal, blurred and sliced by the frame edge
[2 SUBJECT]   — @mira2 stopped mid-knot at the plane branch, the half-made loop lying slack over her fingers, her hand still up at the bark while her head has turned right away toward the far end of the yard
[3 IDENTITY]  @mira2 framed from mid-chest up, nothing below the chest visible — deliberately not standing at rest and not in a full-figure studio pose; her body is angled toward the branch and her face turned fully the other way, the twist visible in the tendon of her neck and the lifted line of her near shoulder. Her mouth is closed and slightly open at one corner, her brows level rather than frowning; her eyes sit just inside the shadow line so they read dark and unlit while her cheek and jaw catch the light. She does not look at the lens. Any skin visible in frame renders matte with low specular and is never tinted green or grey; the reference tones are kept exactly as they are.
[4 SETTING]   The setting is @bahce at the plane branch: the bark in dry cream-and-grey flaking plates immediately behind her hand, the cord crossing it in two slack turns, and beyond her the school's beige stucco flank standing in shade, its base hidden by the raised concrete planter and its low run of shrubs so no flat empty panel of wall is exposed; further off, out of focus, the bench and one pale seated shape too soft to read
[5 LIGHT]     The late-afternoon sun comes through the leaves from the upper right; it takes only her fingers, the slack loop and the near edge of her cheekbone, and it reaches nothing of the stucco flank behind her, which stays a flat cool indigo shade
[7 ALIVE]     Three things are alive in the frame: the free cord end swinging on its diagonal across the foreground, the half-knot easing open by a fibre's width under its own weight, and dappled leaf light sliding over her knuckles.
[8 DEPTH]     Depth in three layers — the swinging cord end blurred and cut across the near foreground, the half-knot and her fingers sharpest of all, her face a hair softer and the shaded wall and distant bench dissolving into gentle bokeh while staying fully modelled.
[9 CONTACT]   Every object rests in contact with its surface and casts a soft contact shadow — the two turns of cord press into the bark and darken a thin crescent under each turn where they bite. Her grip: the cord lies slack across the pads of her index and middle finger, the thumb no longer pressing down on it, and the loop's weight now hangs off her knuckle instead of being held.
STYLE: Feature-animation 3D CGI in the RenderMan / RenderMan-successor premium-CG feature-animation pipeline lineage, subsurface scattering on character skin that carries the scene's light temperature, Every prop has a deliberate overscale factor (10-15%) for child-safe readability, Material specificity is non-negotiable, wood shows visible grain with satin-varnish sheen, fabric shows woven stitch texture and slight fiber fray at edges, complementary bounce fill at 25-35% intensity in the key's opposing hue, FULL 3D CGI FEATURE-ANIMATION RENDER, continuous physically-based shading. Silhouette reads entirely through lighting rim and value separation, Ambient occlusion painted in, never raytraced-hard.

NO acid green, NO neon, NO teal-orange Hollywood grade, NO desaturated grey anywhere in character or environment. Render these as light behaviour, never flat fills.
TEXT: No on-screen text is designed into this frame. [3 IDENTITY]  @mira2 and @dara sit together on the bench, shoulders almost touching, both backs against the slats, framed from the shins up with no shoes and no paving directly beneath them in frame — neither studio pose is imported. @mira2 sits on the left, her hands resting on the slat beside her thigh, her face turned a little toward @dara, mouth closed and easy; around her wrist is a small tassel of twisted red and white cotton thread, the two colours plied around each other in a tight spiral, ending in two short loose brushes of fibre, the knot on the outer side of the wrist bone. @dara sits on the right, hands loose in her lap, chin level, looking out across the yard; a band of leaf shadow crosses the upper half of her face so her eyes read in shade while her mouth and jaw stay lit. Tied on her wrist is a single flat round glass bead in deep cobalt blue with concentric white and pale-blue rings and a dark centre, threaded on a plain waxed cord. All skin in frame renders matte with low specular and is never tinted green or grey; the reference tones are kept exactly as they are.

Behind it the low run of shrubs in their raised concrete planter, worn concrete slabs with grass in the joints, and the beige stucco school building beyond with the flag of Turkey in soft folds; the yard noticeboard by the steps is layered over completely with overlapping sheets of coloured paper and pinned children's paintings so that no bare panel is left anywhere
[5 LIGHT]     A low horizontal late-afternoon light comes in from the left across the yard; it takes only the silvered top slats of the bench and the two pairs of knees, and it reaches neither the plane trunk nor the cast-iron bench legs, which stay a deep cool indigo with no highlight of their own
[7 ALIVE]     Three things are alive in the frame: leaf shadow drifting slowly across the slats behind them, the two brushes of the red-and-white tassel swinging at @mira2's wrist, and the blue bead turning a few degrees on its cord.
[8 DEPTH]     Depth in three layers — the shrub branch blurred and cut at the lower left edge, the bench, the two seated girls and the two wrists sharp across the middle, the plane trunk and the school façade softening into gentle bokeh while staying fully modelled.
[9 CONTACT]   Every object rests in contact with its surface and casts a soft contact shadow — the slats take their weight and each thigh presses a soft dark seam along the board beneath it, and the two cast-iron feet of the bench sit hard into the concrete with a small dark point under each. The two objects: the tassel's knot draws a shallow indentation into @mira2's wrist and its brushes touch her forearm; the waxed cord lies flat around @dara's wrist and the glass bead rests against the skin on the outer side, casting a small round shadow onto it.

### K53 | VO53 "Cevaplarınızı yorumlara bekliyoruz; farklılıklarla kalın, birlikte kalın!" · yazı: YOK · KARAKTER
FİKİR: @mira2 ve @dara çınarın altında, ikisi de doğrudan kameraya bakıyor — filmin TEK doğrudan bakışı. Aralarında @kaval, ikisi de bir ucundan tutuyor. Çalgı ikisinin arasında yatay duruyor, kimsenin değil, ikisinin.
PLAN:  kahraman İKİ UÇTAN TUTULAN TEK ÇALGI · net insan: İKİ, ikisi de yüzüyle ve gövdesiyle (bel hizasından yukarısı) · ışık çınarın yaprakları arasından gelen parçalı sıcak ışık iki yüze ve kavalın gövdesine düşüyor; arkalarındaki bahçe gölgede · ayrım: sıcak parçalı ışık, koyu çınar gövdesinin önünde · FEDA: bir ışık lekesi @dara'nın omzunu patlatıyor, orada kumaş dokusu kayboluyor
-----
[1 LENS]      50mm lens at f/2.8, chest height straight on, medium view with both girls side by side and the flute lying level between them — a cluster of low plane leaves hangs into the upper right foreground, blurred and sliced by the frame edge
[2 SUBJECT]   — one flute held horizontally between two girls, each of them holding one end of it, the tube level and under slight tension between their hands so it belongs to neither of them alone, and both of them looking straight down the lens
[3 IDENTITY]  @mira2 on the left and @dara on the right, both framed from the waist up with no ground and no full-figure studio pose imported, standing close together and turned square to the lens. Both look directly into the camera — this is the one direct look in the film — and both are smiling with their mouths closed, lips together, cheeks lifted. @mira2's chin is level; @dara's is a fraction lower. Between them @kaval lies horizontal across the frame at chest height. All skin in frame renders matte with low specular and is never tinted green or grey; the reference tones are kept exactly as they are.
[4 SETTING]   The setting is directly beneath the mature plane tree in @bahce: the mottled cream-and-grey trunk in dry flaking plates rising close behind them, low leafy branches crowding the top of the frame, the long bench's silvered slats reading edge-on behind their arms, worn concrete slabs and the raised concrete planter's shrubs falling away in shadow, and the beige stucco school building reading only as depth beyond
[5 LIGHT]     A broken dappled warm light comes down through the leaves overhead; it lands on both faces and along the honey-brown body of the flute between them, and it reaches nothing of the plane trunk or the yard behind them, which stay a deep cool indigo with no highlight of their own. One patch of that light falls on @dara's shoulder and burns it to near-white ivory, and the woven texture of her cardigan is lost there.
[7 ALIVE]     Three things are alive in the frame: leaf shadow drifting across both faces, a strand of @mira2's hair lifting on the air, and dust turning in one of the light patches beside the flute.
[8 DEPTH]     Depth in three layers — the low plane leaves blurred and cut at the upper right edge, both faces and the horizontal flute sharp across the middle, the plane trunk, the bench and the school façade softening into gentle bokeh while staying fully modelled.
[9 CONTACT]   Every object rests in contact with its surface and casts a soft contact shadow — the flute lays a soft shadow across the front of each girl's forearm where it crosses. The grip: each girl holds one end of the tube with three fingers curled over the top of it and the thumb braced underneath, their little fingers off the wood, the flute drawn level and taut between the two grips so it neither sags nor tips.
STYLE: Feature-animation 3D CGI in the RenderMan / RenderMan-successor premium-CG feature-animation pipeline lineage, subsurface scattering on character skin that carries the scene's light temperature, Every prop has a deliberate overscale factor (10-15%) for child-safe readability, Material specificity is non-negotiable, wood shows visible grain with satin-varnish sheen, fabric shows woven stitch texture and slight fiber fray at edges, complementary bounce fill at 25-35% intensity in the key's opposing hue, FULL 3D CGI FEATURE-ANIMATION RENDER, continuous physically-based shading. Silhouette reads entirely through lighting rim and value separation, Ambient occlusion painted in, never raytraced-hard.
LIGHT AND PALETTE: shadows read as deep cool indigo, midtones read as vivid warm burnt orange, accents read as vivid warm amber, highlights read as near-white warm ivory — palette character: warm-honey SSS dominant on skin; complementary cool-violet bounce in shadow; wood tones in brown-ochre range with satin sheen; fabric in warm-saturated primaries with fiber texture. NO acid green, NO neon, NO teal-orange Hollywood grade, NO desaturated grey anywhere in character or environment. Render these as light behaviour, never flat fills.
TEXT: No on-screen text is designed into this frame. Every surface in shot that could carry lettering is dressed rather than left bare — panels are layered over with overlapping paper and pinned work, shelves and racks are packed full, cloth is folded or rolled closed, and any remaining surface is given material and grain instead of a blank face, so no empty panel is left where a sign could form. Any background sign is soft-focus, Turkish or blank.
NEGATIVE: nobody is speaking — both mouths stay closed and smiling and neither jaw is open; the flute stays horizontal and held at both ends and is not being offered, played or passed to anyone; exactly two girls are in the frame and no third figure appears behind them; both faces stay modelled and readable and neither flattens into a shape; the bench and the wall behind carry no plaque and no lettering of any kind; NO photoreal or live-action capture and no real-human photographic skin — continuous 3D feature-animation CGI throughout; NO hard-black shadow; NO 2D cel shading; NO hard black outlines; NO flat graphic fill; NO clay/plasticine surface texture on character skin; NO outlines; NO lens flare unless motivated by a visible practical light source in frame; NO anamorphic streak; NO hard cel shading, NO cartoon black outline, NO anime-style eye geometry; NO toon shader, NO 2D illustration style on 3D forms; NO teal-orange Hollywood grade, NO desaturated look, NO high-contrast noir; Turkish label only, NO English signage; NO octane-render aesthetic (avoid: harsh physical sky, raw caustics, overly sharp AO); NO photoreal or live-action capture, NO real-human photographic skin — this is continuous dimensional 3D feature-animation CGI in every scene; no recognizable franchise or real-person characters, logos, brand names; no icon, pictogram, chart, diagram, arrow, gauge or infographic panel standing in for the real object or the real action — the literal thing is IN the frame, rendered as matter, not illustrated as a concept; no empty adjectives (cinematic, dynamic, stunning, 4K); no flat slide; no warped or drifting text; no European or Parisian stone architecture, no desert or orientalist styling — this is contemporary Anatolian
